<h3>Other Artifacts</h3>
<p>Pre-built release artifacts are available in the <strong>Assets</strong> section at the bottom of this page, including:</p>
<ul>
<li>Source distribution tarball</li>
<li>CUDA 12.9 Python wheels for x86_64 and arm64</li>
<li>CUDA 13.0 Python wheels for x86_64 and arm64</li>
<li>CPU Python wheels for x86_64, arm64, and macOS</li>
<li>XPU Python wheel for x86_64</li>
</ul>
<h2>Model Support</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: EmbeddingGemma 2 (<code>google/embeddinggemma-2</code>), an omni-modal text/image/audio/video embedding model, with unused modality encoders skipped through <code>--limit-mm-per-prompt</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60254">#60254</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60289">#60289</a>); MiniCPM-V 4.7 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58674">#58674</a>); Nemotron 3.5 ASR (<code>nvidia/nemotron-3.5-asr-streaming-0.6b</code>) complete-audio transcription; native realtime streaming is not yet supported (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59827">#59827</a>); <code>Cloudflare/clef-omni</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61064">#61064</a>) and <code>LiquidAI/d1-3B</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60676">#60676</a>) on <code>/v1/systemone</code>; PLaMo 3 reasoning and tool parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56575">#56575</a>); and the zerank-2 reranker score template (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60415">#60415</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers modeling backend</strong>: video inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57441">#57441</a>), and Glm, Arcee, CWM, Mellum (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59679">#59679</a>), GPT-NeoX, Phi, Seed-OSS and Jais2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59701">#59701</a>) now served through it.</li>
<li><strong>New model capabilities</strong>: LoRA for BERT, RoBERTa and XLM-RoBERTa embedding models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61033">#61033</a>); Granite as an EAGLE3/DFlash target (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60855">#60855</a>); Qwen3-ASR EAGLE3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52824">#52824</a>); Sarvam MLA EAGLE3/DSpark with PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55902">#55902</a>); GLM-5.3-Flash with the DFlash2 drafter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56983">#56983</a>); and MiniMax-M3 EAGLE3 at PP &gt; 1 on MRV2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57197">#57197</a>) with MTP sharing target embeddings under PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58648">#58648</a>).</li>
<li><strong>GLM-5.3 and GLM-5.3-Flash</strong>: sparse MLA index conversion reused across layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59464">#59464</a>), fused multi-step draft decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57443">#57443</a>), FP8 KV cache default on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60140">#60140</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60281">#60281</a>), <code>fp8_ds_mla</code> on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59246">#59246</a>), qlnorm skipped for MHA (4.4-7.7% TTFT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58845">#58845</a>), long-context indexer prefill sharded across TP (up to 25% lower TTFT at 1M context, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54951">#54951</a>), DCP for the kpool indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59211">#59211</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60032">#60032</a>), KDA prefill checkpoints with FlashKDA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56960">#56960</a>), FP8 KV on SM90 sparse MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55222">#55222</a>), <code>--mamba-ssm-cache-dtype</code> honored for KDA state (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59147">#59147</a>), a generic packed KV layout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57169">#57169</a>), MTP index sharing restored (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61079">#61079</a>), and fixes for vision-tower crashes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59126">#59126</a>), non-square image encoder budgets (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59565">#59565</a>), DP startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56974">#56974</a>), video timestamps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55647">#55647</a>), DFlash2/EAGLE3 aux hidden states (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60763">#60763</a>) and null-block writes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59528">#59528</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4 and V4.1</strong>: decoder-side SWA bounded replay (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58132">#58132</a>) with replay layers in CUDA graphs and trimmed in PIECEWISE graphs (<code>compilation_config.decoder_replay_trim_threshold</code>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59532">#59532</a>) plus last KV-source layer trimming (+3.6%, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59894">#59894</a>), packed FP8 Engram exchange across DP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59923">#59923</a>), faster CPU-offloaded Engram lookups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59327">#59327</a>), fused DeepSeek V4 + MTP drafting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58463">#58463</a>), encoder CUDA graph packing for V4.1 and Kimi-K3 (11% lower mean TTFT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58925">#58925</a>), EPLB for DSpark drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57549">#57549</a>), and fixes for NVFP4 checkpoints with 0% draft acceptance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59655">#59655</a>), MegaMoE shared-expert finalize ordering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59927">#59927</a>), worst-case vision dummy inputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57898">#57898</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/59271">#59271</a>), compressor ring null-block writes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58560">#58560</a>), Triton 3.9 indexer quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60716">#60716</a>) and decoder-replay CUDA graphs with FlashInfer EP MoE backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61041">#61041</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Qwen</strong>: Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (Qwen4Exp) merged QSA projections in one GEMM (1.45-1.68x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59533">#59533</a>), fused QSA pre-indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57097">#57097</a>), sequence parallelism through <code>--enable-hc-sp</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56322">#56322</a>), SM100 low-latency decode GEMM plans (up to 2.7x vs cuBLAS on small-M, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59214">#59214</a>), <code>--kv-cache-dtype-skip-layers</code> honored (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60023">#60023</a>), and PLE loading for INC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59990">#59990</a>), Quark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59443">#59443</a>) and pre-SM89 GPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60142">#60142</a>); Qwen3-VL/Qwen3.5/Qwen3.8 M-RoPE cache 4x smaller and Qwen3.5 YaRN matching Transformers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59853">#59853</a>); Qwen3-Omni M-RoPE fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58890">#58890</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59842">#59842</a>); Qwen3-ASR on the upstream processor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57146">#57146</a>); Qwen3-VL unknown video fps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60501">#60501</a>) and per-video options with the multimodal cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55203">#55203</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kimi-K3 and MiniMax-M3</strong>: Kimi-K3 FlashInfer fused speculative KDA on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54255">#54255</a>), fused vision processor (10-20x preprocessing at 1080p-8K, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59278">#59278</a>), fused K/V pack on the DCP prefill path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56881">#56881</a>), and DSpark KV cache back to 4 groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59733">#59733</a>); MiniMax-M3 MSA prefill with an E4M3 query (26-37% faster with FP8 KV, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60684">#60684</a>), FP8 indexer cache on non-SM100 GPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59081">#59081</a>), Triton indexer tuning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56151">#56151</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59481">#59481</a>), and indexer score fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60194">#60194</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54597">#54597</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal</strong>: device-side input normalization for Qwen3-VL/Qwen3.5/Qwen4Exp (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51289">#51289</a>), GLM-V and GLM-OCR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55389">#55389</a>), Llama Nemotron VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57928">#57928</a>) and Kimi K2.5/K3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59278">#59278</a>), kept fused under <code>compile_mm_encoder</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59195">#59195</a>); encoder inputs sorted by modality (6.5-8.2% more req/s, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60196">#60196</a>); Mistral 3 preprocessing 8% faster (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57531">#57531</a>); Pixtral <code>compile_mm_encoder</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57168">#57168</a>); and fixes for Llama 4 / Mistral Large 3 EAGLE drafters (regression since v0.28.0, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57633">#57633</a>), Phi-4 MM audio OOM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60614">#60614</a>), DeepSeek-OCR zero-sum pixels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59417">#59417</a>), MiMo-V2.5/V2.6 with fastsafetensors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58142">#58142</a>), MiMo-V2.6 FP8 KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58128">#58128</a>), Voxtral realtime (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45022">#45022</a>), Nemotron Super vision LayerNorm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61401">#61401</a>), K-EXAONE config and MTP RoPE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60187">#60187</a>), Nemotron Parse tied <code>lm_head</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60272">#60272</a>), LongCat-Flash MTP loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60080">#60080</a>) and Ling-3.0-flash-VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59026">#59026</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Engine Core</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: GPU n-gram speculator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40704">#40704</a>), Elastic EP with drain-then-commit scaling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53934">#53934</a>), PP with PCP for MLA models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59139">#59139</a>), <code>STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE</code> mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59079">#59079</a>), dynamic K in autoregressive speculators (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57053">#57053</a>), MTP sampling-mask replay (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59359">#59359</a>) and per-module LM heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58921">#58921</a>), non-blocking H2D copies on WSL2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60215">#60215</a>), and fixes for int64 index offsets at long context (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60736">#60736</a>), warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61013">#61013</a>), GDN prefill checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59536">#59536</a>) and MRV2+PP+MTP align state (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60210">#60210</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: fused multi-step draft decode on FlashInfer trtllm-gen (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58371">#58371</a>), FlashInfer ReplaySSM for Mamba2 MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52928">#52928</a>), in-graph metadata for DFlash/DSpark drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60692">#60692</a>), and correctness fixes for ngram on hybrid recurrent models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56531">#56531</a>), Mamba prefill checkpoints with MTP and prefix caching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59759">#59759</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60797">#60797</a>), stop-string trimming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47616">#47616</a>), draft model config (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61240">#61240</a>), adaptive block verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60945">#60945</a>), DSpark bonus slots (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59105">#59105</a>) and draft models on the Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59293">#59293</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Hybrid models and prefix caching</strong>: Mamba2 internal prefill checkpoints for NemotronH, Granite-4.0-H, Falcon-H1 and Zamba2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57329">#57329</a>), stale block hashes dropped when streaming sessions truncate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59103">#59103</a>), and fixes for quantized Mamba-2 with TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58083">#58083</a>), spec decode page sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59975">#59975</a>), prefix-cache bounds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55601">#55601</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60533">#60533</a>) and sm90 Mamba2 NaNs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61718">#61718</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Sleep and RL</strong>: sleep releases workspace scratch (up to 4.5 GiB/GPU, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59156">#59156</a>) and KV-init state (about 4.6 GiB/GPU, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59158">#59158</a>), opt-in <code>sleep_mode_offload_cudagraph</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59160">#59160</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59523">#59523</a>); token-level weight-version spans on outputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59582">#59582</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61070">#61070</a>); weight-transfer communicators released without stopping inference (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61060">#61060</a>); <code>--sleep-preserve-parameter-names 'draft.*'</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61046">#61046</a>); <code>modelexpress</code> weight-transfer backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58399">#58399</a>); <code>POST /weight_checker</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51350">#51350</a>); weight-transfer metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55781">#55781</a>); <code>--enable-nccl-comm-suspend</code> releasing the MNNVL workspace (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59360">#59360</a>); and <code>sleep(level=2)</code> keeping FP8 KV scales (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57163">#57163</a>).</li>
<li><strong>HiSparse</strong>: per-request residency cache (up to 58% at high concurrency with GPU-resident contexts, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60083">#60083</a>), lower scheduler overhead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57930">#57930</a>), GPU residency owned by HiSparse (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60647">#60647</a>), less staging memory (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60429">#60429</a>), host cache gauges (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58949">#58949</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59485">#59485</a>), and fixes for decode token limits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60682">#60682</a>) and TP hangs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60941">#60941</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Startup and memory</strong>: shared-memory tensor arena (<code>--enable-shm-tensor-arena</code>, on by default, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51207">#51207</a>), Fast Start with PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55477">#55477</a>), daemon-held weights counted against <code>gpu_memory_utilization</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57298">#57298</a>), autotune preload (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60085">#60085</a>), local tokenizer snapshots (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60825">#60825</a>), <code>sparse_mla_prefill_workspace_factor</code> saving several GiB at 1M context (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61023">#61023</a>), cached memory reclaimed during weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57516">#57516</a>), fewer Triton sampler warmup compiles (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58605">#58605</a>), sharded state loader with PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52110">#52110</a>), and <code>reload_weights</code> with Run:ai streamer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58124">#58124</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels and runtime</strong>: MoE weighted-sum tuning (1.17-2.37x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59731">#59731</a>), flash-maxsim kernels for ColBERT/ColPali scoring (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40337">#40337</a>), fallback detokenization with 69% less CPU at 32K tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60497">#60497</a>), faster KV block free queue (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60269">#60269</a>), Triton kernels off <code>tl.make_block_ptr</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60502">#60502</a>), fusion passes restored under PyTorch 2.15 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57299">#57299</a>) and static FP8 quant fusion on recent torch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61108">#61108</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Batch invariance</strong>: custom all-reduce under <code>VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58623">#58623</a>), deterministic split-K LoRA shrink (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59377">#59377</a>), every attention backend checked (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60271">#60271</a>), SM120 TP configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58495">#58495</a>), and ROCm support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52231">#52231</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Observability</strong>: <code>vllm:prompt_tokens_cached_by_source_total</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56318">#56318</a>), <code>vllm:num_requests_kv_fetch_by_stage</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58874">#58874</a>), <code>vllm:admission_rejections_total</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55812">#55812</a>), <code>vllm:watermark_requests</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58715">#58715</a>), <code>--custom-histogram-buckets</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48867">#48867</a>), a JSON log formatter via <code>LoggingConfig.formatter</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58739">#58739</a>), and per-session profiling controls (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57875">#57875</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Large Scale Serving</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>MoE communication</strong>: FlashInfer CuteDSL MegaMoE for more models via <code>--moe-backend flashinfer_cutedsl</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54049">#54049</a>), FP8 combine for the FlashInfer one-sided all2all (+4% at DEP8, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57995">#57995</a>), NCCL symmetric-memory MoE reduce-scatter (9.6-16% lower latency, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49194">#49194</a>), DeepEPv2 hybrid NVLink+RDMA mode on by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57991">#57991</a>), Kimi-K3 shared experts with DeepEP v2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56875">#56875</a>), and opt-in EPLB migration batching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52641">#52641</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Context parallelism</strong>: decode requests sharded across PCP ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52162">#52162</a>) and TokenSpeed MLA with block-interleaved DCP for heterogeneous NIXL disaggregation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59462">#59462</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV connectors</strong>: NIXL host-buffer copies coalesced (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54483">#54483</a>), mixed block sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58860">#58860</a>), peer metadata recovery (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55471">#55471</a>) and Qwen3.8-Flash-Next MTP acceptance in P/D (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59997">#59997</a>); Mooncake coalesced hybrid/MLA RDMA regions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57952">#57952</a>) and bootstrap retries (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55923">#55923</a>); MoRIIO Kimi-K3 DSpark READ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57700">#57700</a>); DeepSeek-V4.1 P/D skipping bounded replay for loaded KV (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59197">#59197</a>); and KVCR pool-and-index API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59899">#59899</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Data parallelism</strong>: streaming requests pinned to their engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59017">#59017</a>), per-engine RNG streams (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59788">#59788</a>), and placement groups without <code>ray[default]</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57648">#57648</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Hardware &amp; Performance</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>: native 64-head FlashMLA sparse prefill on SM100 (1.16-1.18x TTFT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60029">#60029</a>), block-FP8 DeepGEMM experts skipping padding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59128">#59128</a>), SM120 split-K attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58482">#58482</a>), DGX Spark Qwen4Exp GEMM plans (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59753">#59753</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59632">#59632</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60027">#60027</a>), OAI Triton MXFP4 MoE on SM12x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58877">#58877</a>), and FlashInfer autotune cache per rank (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57635">#57635</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>: DeepSeek-V4 AITER MegaMoEV2 via <code>--moe-backend aiter_mega_moe</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59685">#59685</a>) with opt-in shared-expert fusion (3-8%, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61004">#61004</a>), opt-in mono decode for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash on MI355X (<code>VLLM_ROCM_MONO_DECODE=1</code>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60397">#60397</a>), opt-in Gluon sparse MLA (up to +16% on GLM-5.2, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53492">#53492</a>), paged MXFP4 indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58671">#58671</a>), opt-in a4w4 MoE for DSv4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58819">#58819</a>), <code>ultraquant_4bit</code> KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57057">#57057</a>), <code>aiter_flydsl</code> GDN prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57560">#57560</a>), Kimi-K3 prefill checkpoints (about 20% better TTFT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58344">#58344</a>), fused MLA decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57640">#57640</a>) and per-rank latent MoE shards (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59591">#59591</a>), GLM-5.3-Flash split-K sparse MLA decode (5-15% lower ITL, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58584">#58584</a>), native <code>merge_attn_states</code> (2.4x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60159">#60159</a>), <code>ROCM_SEGMENTED_ATTN</code> for RDNA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59132">#59132</a>), gfx11 W4A16 tuning (up to 23% TTFT, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56301">#56301</a>), MRV2 default with model-specific MRV1 exceptions removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56360">#56360</a>), and fixes for sleep-mode segfaults on ROCm 7.2+ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60367">#60367</a>) and MLA weight reloads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60389">#60389</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>: MLA in <code>FLASH_ATTN_MLA</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58297">#58297</a>), Arc Pro B70 FP8 GEMM (2.0-2.73x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56063">#56063</a>) and fused MoE configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53065">#53065</a>), fused SYCL LayerNorm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57172">#57172</a>), and fixes for Gemma-2 softcap (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58936">#58936</a>) and DP+EP MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57787">#57787</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>: vectorized sampler (up to 58x on s390x, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53913">#53913</a>), aarch64 causal conv1d (about 9%, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54093">#54093</a>), POWER10 AWQ/GPTQ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59149">#59149</a>), and PowerPC <code>dtype=auto</code> choosing bfloat16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58528">#58528</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Other</strong>: tpu-inference v0.31.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60190">#60190</a>) and out-of-tree platforms registering multiple attention backends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41091">#41091</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Quantization</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New capabilities</strong>: online re-quantization of linear layers (<code>--quantization-config.linear fp8_per_channel</code>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55684">#55684</a>), per-token NVFP4 CuTe-DSL MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50030">#50030</a>) with ReLU2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56740">#56740</a>), MXFP4 MoE oracle with CUTLASS W4A4 and BF16 fallback (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60079">#60079</a>), compressed-tensors MXFP4 MoE with FP8 activations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59815">#59815</a>), ModelOpt IQ2_XS/IQ2_XXS/Q8_0 on SM120/SM121 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59604">#59604</a>), <code>int4_per_token_head</code> KV with non-power-of-two heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56198">#56198</a>), and Humming preferred over Marlin on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56997">#56997</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fixes</strong>: silent CUTLASS NVFP4/MXFP4 MoE corruption under EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59042">#59042</a>), GPTQ with non-default safetensors names (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60411">#60411</a>), <code>moe_wna16</code> 8-bit asymmetric zero points (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58950">#58950</a>), and ModelOpt mixed-precision NVFP4 detection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56050">#56050</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>API &amp; Frontend</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New endpoints and options</strong>: <code>/v1/systemone</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59299">#59299</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60677">#60677</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60696">#60696</a>), <code>hf</code> parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58604">#58604</a>), <code>vllm download-kernels</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58765">#58765</a>), <code>output_mode: "text"</code> and <code>stop_reason</code> in <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58588">#58588</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60251">#60251</a>), <code>mm_payload</code> for render requests (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61427">#61427</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60728">#60728</a>), Responses <code>min_p</code> and <code>stop_token_ids</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48084">#48084</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56592">#56592</a>), <code>LLM.chat(priority=...)</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57243">#57243</a>), and <code>/generative_scoring</code> <code>cache_salt</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60841">#60841</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Behavior</strong>: <code>max_tokens</code> is an upper bound instead of a reservation, so long prompts are no longer rejected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57035">#57035</a>); least-loaded API worker selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60723">#60723</a>); tool parser compatibility checked at startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59749">#59749</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Tool calling and structured output</strong>: <code>parallel_tool_calls=false</code> enforced (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50502">#50502</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59298">#59298</a>), <code>tool_choice="required"</code> streaming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60354">#60354</a>) and native XML parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47512">#47512</a>), Step-3.5 and <code>minimax_m3</code> on the parser engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59321">#59321</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59743">#59743</a>), xgrammar <code>propertyNames</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57594">#57594</a>), and LoRA PEFT <code>rank_pattern</code>/<code>alpha_pattern</code> applied with unsupported features rejected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59801">#59801</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60225">#60225</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong>: <code>--grpc-port</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59659">#59659</a>), <code>--stream-interval</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60589">#60589</a>), <code>logprob_token_ids</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59549">#59549</a>), <code>Control.Shutdown</code> with <code>vllm-proto</code> 0.5.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59316">#59316</a>), <code>qwen3_json</code> parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60136">#60136</a>), and Kimi K3 grammar fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59190">#59190</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59395">#59395</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Security</h2>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-4hhp-h66f-j5j7" title="GHSA-4hhp-h66f-j5j7">GHSA-4hhp-h66f-j5j7</a>: chat templates are validated statically and rendered under a timeout (<code>VLLM_CHAT_TEMPLATE_RENDER_TIMEOUT</code>), user-supplied <code>chat_template</code> in Responses <code>chat_template_kwargs</code> is rejected, and the Rust frontend caps template execution (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50300">#50300</a>).</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-q97c-p368-75c4" title="GHSA-q97c-p368-75c4">GHSA-q97c-p368-75c4</a>: tokenizer <code>max_token_id</code> off-by-one fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59491">#59491</a>).</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-frvg-qw88-hww9" title="GHSA-frvg-qw88-hww9">GHSA-frvg-qw88-hww9</a>: P2P ZMQ peer sessions are bounded by <code>VLLM_P2P_MAX_PEERS</code> (default 128), <code>VLLM_P2P_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_S</code> (default 30) and <code>VLLM_P2P_IDLE_TIMEOUT_S</code> (default 300), so unique peer addresses can no longer exhaust ZMQ sockets (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51504">#51504</a>).</li>
<li>Default CORS settings no longer allow every origin in the Python and Rust frontends; set <code>--allowed-origins</code> to permit browser origins (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61009">#61009</a>).</li>
<li>Untrusted images are opened with a raster-format allowlist (<code>ALLOWED_IMAGE_FORMATS</code>), denying EPS/PostScript and WMF (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60022">#60022</a>); shared-memory multimodal cache handles are HMAC-signed and forged handles rejected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59357">#59357</a>).</li>
<li>Structured-output JSON schemas nested more than 64 levels return 400 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60036">#60036</a>); chunked <code>/v1/embeddings</code> requests keep <code>cache_salt</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47696">#47696</a>); Phi-4 MM audio no longer holds multi-GiB attention matrices (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60614">#60614</a>); DeepSeek-OCR zero-sum pixel tensors no longer kill EngineCore (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59417">#59417</a>).</li>
<li>Security dependency bumps: <code>aiohttp&gt;=3.14.3</code>, <code>pillow&gt;=12.3.0</code>, cryptography, urllib3, pyjwt and others (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59249">#59249</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59315">#59315</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59427">#59427</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Dependencies</h2>
<ul>
<li><code>transformers&gt;=5.16.1,&lt;5.20.0</code> (minimum raised from 5.10.4, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57387">#57387</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59762">#59762</a>) and <code>tokenizers&gt;=0.23.2</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59796">#59796</a>).</li>
<li>NIXL 1.5.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56907">#56907</a>), CuTe DSL 4.8.0 block-scale APIs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59480">#59480</a>), <code>humming-kernels==0.1.17</code> with initial Rubin support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61556">#61556</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61858">#61858</a>), InstantTensor 0.2.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61167">#61167</a>), and <code>flashinfer-jit-cache</code> excluded from wheel <code>install_requires</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60052">#60052</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: AITER 0.1.24.post1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59794">#59794</a>) and MoRI v1.2.3.post1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56073">#56073</a>). XPU: auto-round-lib 0.16.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60976">#60976</a>). TPU: tpu-inference v0.31.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60190">#60190</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Build</h2>
<ul>
<li>Python 3.11-3.14 supported (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60402">#60402</a>); macOS wheels built with the stable ABI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60312">#60312</a>); CUDA source builds require GCC 13 and fail early otherwise (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60259">#60259</a>); Docker images compile Python bytecode at build time for faster container startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55422">#55422</a>); <code>VLLM_USE_PRECOMPILED</code> finds a compatible published wheel within the last 20 commits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58871">#58871</a>); the vendored DeepGEMM uses <code>TORCH_LIBRARY</code> (abi3, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48962">#48962</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Breaking Changes &amp; Deprecations</h2>
<ul>
<li>Python 3.10 is no longer supported; the minimum is Python 3.11 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60402">#60402</a>).</li>
<li>Default CORS origins changed from <code>*</code> to none; set <code>--allowed-origins</code> for browser clients (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61009">#61009</a>).</li>
<li><code>transformers&gt;=5.16.1,&lt;5.20.0</code> and <code>tokenizers&gt;=0.23.2</code> are required (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57387">#57387</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59796">#59796</a>).</li>
<li>The torch.compile sequence parallelism and AsyncTP passes were removed, along with <code>PassConfig.enable_sp</code>, <code>fuse_gemm_comms</code> and <code>sp_min_token_num</code>; model-level sequence parallelism is unaffected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60517">#60517</a>).</li>
<li>Cascade attention was removed; <code>--disable-cascade-attn</code> still parses but only warns and will be removed in v0.33.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61126">#61126</a>).</li>
<li><code>mamba_cache_mode="all"</code> was removed; <code>--mamba-cache-mode all</code> now fails validation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58997">#58997</a>).</li>
<li>Previously deprecated APIs were removed: <code>wait_for_inflight_requests</code> (use <code>mode="wait"</code>), <code>AsyncLLM.pause_generation()</code>, <code>IOProcessor.output_to_response</code>, <code>vllm.scripts.main()</code> and <code>vllm bench throughput --dataset</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60923">#60923</a>). The <code>sonnet</code> benchmark dataset was removed as scheduled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60129">#60129</a>). Tensorizer support was removed from LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60024">#60024</a>), and the PyO3 <code>vllm._rust_tool_parser</code> bridge was removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59744">#59744</a>).</li>
<li>API changes: <code>max_tokens</code> is treated as an upper bound on output rather than reserved from the context window (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57035">#57035</a>); Responses API streaming uses <code>response.content_part.added/done</code> with <code>part.type="reasoning_text"</code> instead of <code>response.reasoning_part.added/done</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59652">#59652</a>); <code>SamplingParams.prompt_logprob_token_ids</code> is per row (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56984">#56984</a>); <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> logprobs use integer token IDs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58181">#58181</a>); sleep API responses are standardized and invalid levels return 400 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52864">#52864</a>); <code>return_mm_kwargs</code> is deprecated in favor of <code>mm_payload</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61427">#61427</a>).</li>
<li>Watermarking: when the engine has watermarking configured, an omitted request field means enabled and only <code>false</code> opts out; unsupported combinations warn and fall back (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56801">#56801</a>).</li>
<li>Rust frontend: <code>qwen3_xml</code> now parses Qwen3-Coder XML (alias of <code>qwen3_coder</code>); use the new <code>qwen3_json</code> for the JSON format (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60136">#60136</a>).</li>
<li>New defaults: shared-memory tensor arena on (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51207">#51207</a>); GLM-5.x FP8 KV cache on SM100 with <code>--kv-cache-dtype auto</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60140">#60140</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60281">#60281</a>); <code>VLLM_DEEPEP_V2_ALLOW_HYBRID_MODE=1</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57991">#57991</a>); Humming preferred over Marlin on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56997">#56997</a>); <code>sparse_mla_prefill_workspace_factor</code> 2 above 256K <code>max_model_len</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61023">#61023</a>); MRV2 on ROCm with model-specific MRV1 exceptions removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56360">#56360</a>); <code>VLLM_ROCM_AITER_MLA_DCP_VERIFY=auto</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59965">#59965</a>); <code>dtype=auto</code> picks bfloat16 on PowerPC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58528">#58528</a>); MXFP4 W4A4 checkpoints fall back to W4A16 with a warning when no W4A4 kernel exists (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60079">#60079</a>).</li>
<li>Stricter validation: incompatible <code>--prefix-match-unit</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58021">#58021</a>), <code>max_num_scheduled_tokens=0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60714">#60714</a>), HiSparse with <code>cudagraph_mode=FULL</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59688">#59688</a>), and negative <code>--device-ids</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49053">#49053</a>) are rejected at startup.</li>
</ul>

## Operating Systems

### [OpenSUSE Leap 16.1](https://lwn.net/Articles/1105131/)

**来源**: LWN.net (Linux Weekly News)

**摘要**: <p>
The openSUSE project has <a href="https://news.opensuse.org/2026/11/03/leap-unites-traditional-and-immutable/">announced</a> the release of openSUSE Leap 16.1. The release is notable for allowing both traditional installations and immutable images with transactional updates. While immutable images had previously been available for Leap Micro, they are now available across all platforms that Leap supports, including servers, desktops, and virtual machines.
</p>

<blockquote class="bq">
"Leap's image gives the best of both for traditional and immutable users, and we expect that Tumbleweed will follow when Agama becomes the default installer for Tumbleweed as well." said Leap release manager Lubos Kocman. "The Leap Micro experience people relied on is still here, but now it shares the same release, the same life cycle and the same maintenance updates as the rest of Leap, so users only have to pick the mode that fits their workload."
</blockquote>

---

### [[$] LLM reviews for Debian's new queue spark debate](https://lwn.net/Articles/1103674/)

**来源**: LWN.net (Linux Weekly News)

**摘要**: <p>The Debian project has wrestled with the topic of LLM usage for years, which
culminated in a <a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1087134/">general-resolution
vote</a> this August. The project <a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1091231/">voted in favor</a> of the fifth, out of
eight, option: "<a href="https://www.debian.org/vote/2026/vote_002#texte">Responsible Use of
Generative AI</a>". With the door open to LLM use, the <a href="https://wiki.debian.org/Teams/DFSG">DFSG, Licensing &amp; New Packages
Team</a> (usually shortened to "DFSG team"), which is <a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1043364/">relatively new</a>, has started to employ LLMs in
sending out reviews of packages entering Debian's <a href="https://wiki.debian.org/NewQueue?pow_referer=https%3A%2F%2Fkagi.com%2F">new queue</a>. The practice has
raised a number of objections and questions about where lines should be drawn in
the use of LLM-generated output.</p>

---

### [Fedora Linux 45 released](https://lwn.net/Articles/1105121/)

**来源**: LWN.net (Linux Weekly News)

**摘要**: <p><a href="https://fedoramagazine.org/announcing-fedora-linux-45/">Fedora Linux
45</a> has been released. This includes all editions: <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/workstation/">Fedora Workstation</a>, <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/kde/">Fedora KDE Plasma Desktop</a>, <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/server/">Fedora Server</a>, <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/cloud/">Fedora Cloud</a>, <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/coreos/">Fedora CoreOS</a>, and <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/iot/">Fedora IoT</a>, as well as the <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/atomic-desktops/">Atomic Desktops</a> and <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/spins/">Spins</a>.</p>

<p>This release adds support for <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/wiki/Releases/45/ChangeSet#oo7_Secrets_Service_Provider">installation
on Stratis storage</a> using Anaconda or Kickstart installs, <a href="https://fedoramagazine.org/migrating-away-from-unmaintained-networkmanager-vpnpluginsvpnc-and-fortisslvpn/">retires
unmaintained VPN plugins</a> for NetworkManager, and updates Fedora's desktop
environments, developer toolchains, and system packages. See the <a href="https://fedoraproject.org/wiki/Releases/45/ChangeSet">Fedora wiki
ChangeSet page</a> for a list of major changes.</p>

<p></p>

---
